country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.301
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_state_nomadic.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			blocks_minister_of_state = no is_gestalt = no is_nomadic = yes
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_state_nomadic } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_state_nomadic
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_state_nomadic }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_state_nomadic } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_state_nomadic
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_state_nomadic
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_state_nomadic
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			blocks_minister_of_state = no is_gestalt = no is_nomadic = yes

		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.301 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

			limit = {

				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_state
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_state
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {

			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_anglers } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_anglers
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_anglers
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_anglers
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_anglers
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_anglers } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1001 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1003
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_ascensionists.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_ascensionists
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_ascensionists } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_ascensionists
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_ascensionists }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_ascensionists } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_ascensionists
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_ascensionists
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_ascensionists
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_corporate_dominion
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}

			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_corporate_dominion } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"

				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }

			}

		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1009 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1011
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_crafters.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no

				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

				is_pool_leader = no

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_crafters
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_crafters
			}
		}

	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1013 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1014
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_death_cult.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_death_cult
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_death_cult } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_death_cult
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_death_cult }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_death_cult } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_death_cult
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_death_cult
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_death_cult
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_death_cult
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_death_cult } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {

				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_efficient_bureaucracy
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_efficient_bureaucracy
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_efficient_bureaucracy
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_efficient_bureaucracy } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_efficient_bureaucracy
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_efficient_bureaucracy }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_efficient_bureaucracy } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_efficient_bureaucracy
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_efficient_bureaucracy
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_efficient_bureaucracy
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_efficient_bureaucracy
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_efficient_bureaucracy } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_efficient_bureaucracy
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_efficient_bureaucracy } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1018 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1020
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_exalted_priesthood.pick_name"

	is_triggered_only = yes

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_functional_architecture
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_functional_architecture } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_functional_architecture
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_functional_architecture }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_functional_architecture } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_functional_architecture
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_functional_architecture
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_functional_architecture
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_functional_architecture
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_functional_architecture } }

		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1024 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_imperial_cult
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_imperial_cult
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_imperial_cult
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_imperial_cult
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_imperial_cult } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_imperial_cult
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_imperial_cult }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_imperial_cult } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_imperial_cult
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_imperial_cult
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_imperial_cult
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_imperial_cult
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_imperial_cult } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1027 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1029
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_memorialist.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_memorialist
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_memorialist } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_memorialist
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_memorialist }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_memorialist } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_memorialist
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_memorialist
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_memorialist
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}

				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_selective_kinship } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_selective_kinship
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_selective_kinship
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_selective_kinship
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_selective_kinship
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_selective_kinship } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1053 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1058
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_task_delegation_corporate.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_task_delegation_corporate
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_task_delegation_corporate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_task_delegation_corporate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_task_delegation_corporate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_task_delegation_corporate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_task_delegation_corporate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_task_delegation_corporate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_task_delegation_corporate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_corporate_anglers
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_corporate_anglers
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}

	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_death_cult_corporate
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_death_cult_corporate } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1068 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1069
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_free_traders.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_free_traders
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_free_traders } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_free_traders
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_free_traders }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_free_traders } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_free_traders
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_free_traders
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_free_traders
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_free_traders
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_free_traders } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。

			has_valid_civic = civic_media_conglomerate

		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_media_conglomerate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_media_conglomerate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_media_conglomerate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_media_conglomerate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_media_conglomerate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_media_conglomerate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned

				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_media_conglomerate

				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1072 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {

	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {

		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no

			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no

				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }

			}

			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1084
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_gestalt_growth.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			is_gestalt = yes
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_gestalt_growth } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_gestalt_growth
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_gestalt_growth }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_gestalt_growth } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_gestalt_growth
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_gestalt_growth
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_gestalt_growth
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			is_gestalt = yes
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_gestalt_growth } }
		}

		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			any_owned_leader = {
				has_trait = leader_trait_shroudwalker
			}
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_shroudwalker_teacher } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_shroudwalker_teacher
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_shroudwalker_teacher }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_shroudwalker_teacher } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_shroudwalker_teacher
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_shroudwalker_teacher
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_shroudwalker_teacher
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			any_owned_leader = {
				has_trait = leader_trait_shroudwalker
			}
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_shroudwalker_teacher } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_shroudwalker_teacher
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {

				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_shroudwalker_teacher } }

			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {

		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				any_owned_leader = {
				has_trait = leader_trait_shroudwalker
			}
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_shroudwalker_teacher } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1096 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1138
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_chosen_corporate.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_chosen
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_chosen_corporate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_chosen_corporate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_chosen_corporate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_chosen_corporate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_chosen_corporate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_chosen_corporate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_chosen_corporate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_chosen
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_chosen_corporate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_2"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_chosen_corporate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_chosen_corporate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_chosen_corporate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_chosen_corporate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_chosen_corporate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_chosen_corporate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_2 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_chosen
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_chosen_corporate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_chosen_corporate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_chosen_corporate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_chosen_corporate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_chosen_corporate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_chosen_corporate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_chosen_corporate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_chosen
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_chosen_corporate } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_chosen_corporate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_chosen_corporate }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_chosen_corporate } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_chosen_corporate
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_chosen_corporate
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_chosen_corporate
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_corporate_chosen
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_chosen_corporate } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1138 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_3"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_deep_sleep
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_deep_sleep }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_deep_sleep } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_deep_sleep
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_deep_sleep
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_deep_sleep
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_3 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_deep_sleep
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_deep_sleep } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_deep_sleep
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_deep_sleep }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_deep_sleep } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_deep_sleep
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_deep_sleep
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_deep_sleep
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_deep_sleep
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_deep_sleep } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1141 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1145
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_waystation_quota_auditor.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_caravan_masters_corporate
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_waystation_quota_auditor } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_waystation_quota_auditor
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_waystation_quota_auditor }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_waystation_quota_auditor } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_waystation_quota_auditor
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_waystation_quota_auditor
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_waystation_quota_auditor
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_SH_yihui
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_SH_yihui } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_SH_yihui
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_SH_yihui }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_SH_yihui } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_SH_yihui
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_SH_yihui
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_SH_yihui
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }

		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1151 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_wgelves
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_wg_elves } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_4"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_wg_elves
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_wg_elves }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_wg_elves } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_wg_elves
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_wg_elves
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_wg_elves
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_4 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
				NOT = {
				has_valid_civic = civic_wgelves
				NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_wg_elves } }
				}
			}
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.no_candidate"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.999 }
	}
	# 刷新换一批
	option = {
		trigger = { any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 } }
		name = "auto_qol_assign.refresh"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.1210 }
	}
	# 返回分类子菜单
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol_assign.back_to_cat"
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.30 }
	}

	# 离开并关闭界面 (支持右上角X与ESC)
	option = {
		name = "auto_qol.close_direct"
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = auto_qol_council_assign.1211
	title = "auto_qol_council_assign.councilor_wg_sunflower.pick_name"
	desc = "auto_qol_council_assign.pick_desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_1
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_2
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_3
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_pool_leader = no
				is_ruler = no
				is_councilor = no
				NOT = { exists = fleet }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_2 }
				NOT = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_3 }
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_4
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_cand_4
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_leader = { has_leader_flag = auto_qol_cand_flag_1 }
			has_valid_civic = civic_sunflower
			NOT = { has_councilor = { COUNCILOR = councilor_wg_sunflower } }
		}
		name = "auto_qol_assign.cand_1"
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			# 优先复用原版 UI 或本 Mod 已经创建的空席位。
			set_council_position = councilor_wg_sunflower
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
					NOT = { is_councilor_type = councilor_wg_sunflower }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_wg_sunflower } }
				set_country_flag = auto_qol_slot_councilor_wg_sunflower
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_wg_sunflower
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_wg_sunflower
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
	}

				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_uf_2
			}
			event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
				set_council_position = councilor_uf_2
			}
		}
		event_target:auto_qol_cand_1 = {
			save_event_target_as = auto_qol_last_assigned
		}
		auto_qol_clear_council_candidate_flags = yes
		country_event = { id = auto_qol_council_assign.99 }
